- **deuxui**: UX engineering with a verdict, from the first wireframe to the release gate. Two review stages a human actually uses: /ux-comp renders wireframes from the declared contract and /ux-decide serves them as a page, then /ux-proto generates a working prototype — real states, a form that validates, a dialog that traps focus, an undo that works — and /ux-review hosts it live beside the current product so a person can Alt-click any element and say what is wrong in their own words, which lands as a work item the agent reads. Plus /ux (the router), /ux-phase, /ux-select, /ux-fonts, /ux-init, /ux-create… ([kryptobaseddev/awesome-skills](https://github.com/kryptobaseddev/awesome-skills/tree/HEAD/deuxui-plugin) · 1 skill, 40 commands, 3 agents, 1 hook file)
